# BG-04 — BULGARIAN IMAGE-TEXT UNIT LEDGER

Phase: **BG-04 — IMAGE-TEXT INVENTORY (ACTIVE)**
Scope: **47 pages / 47 graphics**
Authority: immutable final Italian PDF for identity, position, and visible wording; editable upstream sources only where directly matched.
Method: direct 300 dpi inspection in ten controlled batches (maximum five graphics). OCR was not used as textual authority. Native page captions outside artwork are excluded.

## Unit-record rules

- IDs are stable and unique in the form `Pxxx-GA-U001`.
- `T` translates verified Italian; `R` retains documentary/proper-name content; `N` records numeric/symbolic content; `U` isolates wording that cannot safely be promoted from the former aggregate record.
- A `U` record has no invented Bulgarian target. Its diagnostic render and the exact missing evidence are recorded.
- PAGE 16 is preserved verbatim as the approved structural model.

Proteggerle significa garantire uno sviluppo armonico e sano. | T | Растежните хрущяли са участъци от хрущялна тъкан в краищата на дългите кости.
